  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数研究聚焦于基相关的相干性度量，而基无关的内在相干性动力学在开放量子系统中尚未被充分探索。

Method: 研究了多种代表性三体纯态和混合态在局域和共同退相位环境（马尔可夫与非马尔可夫区）下的基无关相干性动力学，并与相对熵相干性度量进行对比。

Result: 马尔可夫局域退相位导致态依赖的相干性退化；共同退相位通过无退相干子空间显著增强相干保持；非马尔可夫环境下纯态和混合态的相干性近乎冻结；基无关相干性比相对熵相干性具有更强的鲁棒性和定性不同的动力学行为。

Conclusion: 基无关相干性是一种鲁棒的量子资源，在真实噪声量子技术中具有重要潜力，为开放量子系统中内在多体相干性的保持提供了新见解。

Abstract: Quantum coherence is a fundamental quantum resource whose preservation under environmental interactions is essential for quantum information processing. While most studies have focused on basis-dependent coherence measures, the dynamics of intrinsic coherence quantified by basis-independent measures remain largely unexplored. In this work, we investigate the dynamics of basis-independent quantum coherence for several representative tripartite pure and mixed states subjected to local and common dephasing environments in both Markovian and non-Markovian regimes. We show that Markovian local dephasing leads to state-dependent coherence degradation, whereas collective dephasing significantly enhances coherence preservation through decoherence-free sectors. More importantly, non-Markovian environments give rise to nearly frozen coherence dynamics for both pure and mixed states, demonstrating the remarkable robustness of intrinsic coherence against dephasing environment. A comparison with the measure of relative entropy of coherence reveals that basis-independent coherence measure exhibits substantially greater resilience and qualitatively different dynamical behaviour than its basis-dependent counterpart. These results provide new insights into the preservation of intrinsic multipartite coherence in open quantum systems and highlight basis-independent coherence as a robust quantum resource for realistic noisy quantum technologies.

</details>


### [94] [Security Evaluation of Laser-Phase-Noise Quantum Random Number Generators with Intrinsic Correlations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13521)
*Y. Gao,J. H Li,J. L. Liu,M. Wu,L. J. Liu,H. Wang,Y. Pan,W. Huang,Y. Li,J. Yang,B. J. Xu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了量子随机数发生器（QRNG）中激光相位噪声本征相关性的分析模型，给出了存在本征相关性时条件最小熵的解析表达式，精确量化了实际可提取的随机性。数值模拟和实验验证了模型的有效性，结果表明忽略本征相关性会导致可提取随机性被高估约46%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有激光相位噪声型量子随机数发生器常忽略原始数据中的本征相关性，违背了独立同分布假设，可能危及系统安全性。需要建立严格的理论模型来精确量化实际可提取的随机性，确保信息论安全性。

Method: 建立了本征相关性的分析模型，推导了存在本征相关性时条件最小熵的解析表达式，并通过数值模拟和实验对理论模型进行验证。

Result: 理论模型与数值模拟及实验结果高度一致；在典型设置下，忽略本征相关性会导致可提取随机性被高估约46%。

Conclusion: 该工作为设计具有严格安全性分析的紧凑、高性能量子随机数发生器提供了有价值的理论框架。

Abstract: Quantum random number generators are essential for achieving information-theoretical security in modern cryptographic systems. Among various implementations, laser phase noise schemes are widely favored for their simple architecture and high integration potential. However, the intrinsic correlations in the raw data are often neglected, which violates the independent and identically distributed assumption and potentially compromises system security. In this work, we establish an analytical model of correlation and formulate an analytical expression for the conditional min-entropy in the presence of intrinsic correlations to accurately quantify the genuinely extractable randomness. The validity of our theoretical model is confirmed by numerical simulations and experimental results, exhibiting excellent agreement. Under typical setups it is shown that neglecting intrinsic correlations leads to an overestimation of extractable randomness by approximately 46%. This work provides a valuable theoretical framework for designing compact, high-performance quantum random number generators with rigorous security analysis.

</details>


### [95] [Tensor Network decoding under inter-qubit correlated errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13570)
*Yue Yan,SiYing Wang,ZhiXin Xia,HanNuo Yuan,CanWei Shi,Xiang-Bin Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于多索引张量网络的最大似然译码器，用于处理量子表面码中的量子比特间相关错误模型。通过引入额外二进制索引和变换构造多索引张量网络，并利用重参数化、消除和索引分类降低张量维度，实现了高效可收缩的张量网络，在相关错误模型下获得了比MWPM译码器更高的有限尺寸阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有张量网络译码器通常针对独立单量子比特错误模型设计，其在量子比特间相关错误模型下的性能尚未被探索。主要面临两大挑战：一是相关错误下难以构造张量网络，因为相同最终泡利错误可由多种独立和相关错误组合产生；二是构造出的张量网络往往包含高维张量，无法高效收缩。

Method: 引入额外二进制索引和两种变换来构造多索引张量网络；使用重参数化、消除和索引分类将高维张量分解为低维张量，从而得到可高效收缩的张量网络。

Result: 在代表性相关错误模型下，基于多索引张量网络的最大似然译码器比广泛使用的MWPM译码器实现了更高的有限尺寸阈值。

Conclusion: 本文提出的方法成功将张量网络最大似然译码器扩展到相关错误模型，解决了构造和收缩两大难题，为量子纠错中的相关错误译码提供了有效方案。

Abstract: The maximum likelihood decoder based on tensor networks has proven highly successful for the 2D surface code, achieving the optimal decoding success rate. However, existing tensor network decoders are typically designed for independent single-qubit error models, and their performance under inter-qubit correlated error models remains unexplored. This is due to two major challenges. The first challenge lies in constructing the tensor network for correlated errors, since the same final Pauli error can arise from many different combinations of independent and correlated errors, preventing a direct factorization of the error probability. The second challenge is that even after a tensor network is constructed, it generally contains huge-dimensional tensors and is therefore not efficiently contractible. In this work, to address the first difficulty, we introduce additional binary indices and two transformations to construct a multi-index tensor network for maximum-likelihood decoding with correlated errors. To address the second difficulty, we use reparametrization, elimination, and index classification to decompose the huge-dimensional tensors into lower-dimensional tensors. This yields an efficiently contractible tensor network for error models satisfying the tractability conditions derived in this work. We perform numerical simulations for a representative correlated error model and show that the maximum-likelihood decoder implemented with our multi-index tensor network construction achieves a higher finite-size threshold than the widely used MWPM decoder.

</details>


### [96] [Filon Methods for Highly Oscillatory Controlled Quantum Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13580)
*Spencer Lee,Daniel Appelö*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对量子系统模拟中高度振荡动力学带来的数值计算效率问题，提出了两种基于Filon积分的数值方法（Filon和Controlled Filon）用于求解高度振荡的常微分方程线性系统。Controlled Filon方法还考虑了控制脉冲的振荡结构。数值实验表明，这两种方法显著减少了模拟超导transmon量子比特系统所需的计算成本，在CNOT门动力学模拟中，Controlled Filon方法在所有目标精度下都是最有效的，比同阶Hermite方法快多达500倍，比最优Hermite方法快多达6倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典模拟量子系统在量子计算机的设计与控制中至关重要，但量子系统的高度振荡动力学严重限制了标准数值方法的效率。

Method: 将Filon求积法适配到数值求解高度振荡ODE线性系统中，提出Filon方法和Controlled Filon方法。两种方法针对受控量子系统的高效实现进行了定制，Controlled Filon方法额外考虑了控制脉冲的振荡结构。

Result: 数值实验表明，两种方法显著减少了模拟超导transmon量子比特系统的时间步数，以适度增加每步计算成本为代价达到给定精度。在CNOT门模拟中，Controlled Filon方法在所有精度下均为最优，比同阶Hermite方法快500倍，比最佳Hermite方法快6倍。

Conclusion: 所提出的Filon类方法能有效降低高振荡量子系统经典模拟的计算成本，特别适用于受控量子系统的仿真场景。

Abstract: Fast and accurate classical simulation of quantum systems is a central challenge in the design and control of quantum computers, but the highly oscillatory dynamics of these systems severely limit the efficiency of standard numerical methods. To address this, we adapt Filon quadrature for oscillatory integrals into two numerical methods, called Filon and Controlled Filon, for solving linear systems of ODEs with highly oscillatory solutions. We tailor both methods for efficient implementation in controlled quantum systems, and the Controlled Filon method additionally accounts for the oscillatory structure of the control pulses. We show by numerical experiments that these methods significantly reduce the computational cost of accurately simulating systems of superconducting transmon qubits by decreasing the number of timesteps needed to reach a given level of precision, with only a modest increase in the cost per timestep. For a realistic simulation of the dynamics of a CNOT gate, the Controlled Filon method is the most efficient method tested at every target accuracy, outperforming the best Hermite method by up to 6x and the Hermite method of the same order by up to 500x.

</details>


### [97] [The potential of quantum computers for Particle Image Velocimetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13641)
*Philipp Pfeffer,Theo Käufer,Julia Ingelmann,Christian Cierpka,Jörg Schumacher*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于量子傅里叶变换的量子粒子图像测速（QuPIV）算法，替代传统PIV中依赖FFT的互相关计算，通过新颖的状态制备、改进的振幅放大和输出提取等端到端设计，在合成与实验数据上验证了可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统PIV通过FFT计算数百万个速度矢量，计算开销巨大；利用量子傅里叶变换的指数级加速潜力，降低PIV的计算复杂度。

Method: 提出端到端量子算法QuPIV：包含新颖的状态制备方法、改进的振幅放大（采用收缩基态投影子减少量子门数量）、以及输出提取模块；用多维量子傅里叶变换替代经典FFT互相关计算。

Result: 在合成数据与实验数据上对所有算法阶段进行了数值验证，证明了端到端的可行性。

Conclusion: QuPIV有望在保持亚像素精度的前提下，显著降低大规模PIV速度场计算的计算资源需求，为流场测量提供量子加速方案。

Abstract: Particle Image Velocimetry (PIV) is the prime image-processing technique to measure and visualize velocity fields of laminar and turbulent flows. The velocity field vectors are obtained with sub-pixelaccuracy by analyzing cross-correlations, empowered by Fast Fourier Transforms (FFT). Here, we present a quantum algorithm with multidimensional quantum Fourier Transforms, termed Quantum-based PIV (QuPIV), to replace the classical computation of up to millions of velocity vectors. Our end-to-end quantum algorithm includes a novel state preparation, modified amplitude amplification, and the output extraction. We enhance amplitude amplification by a contracted ground-state projector, which allows a significant reduction of the number of gates in the quantum circuit. We justify the end-to-end capability with numerical studies on all stages of the algorithm on both synthetic and experimental data.

</details>


### [98] [Effects of coherent and incoherent measurement imperfections on multipartite quantum nonlocality and quantum key distribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13645)
*Qiong Wang,Wen-Long Qiao,Qing Chen,Liu-Jun Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文分析了多体GHZ态中测量误差（相干角度偏差和非相干结果翻转）对Mermin、Svetlichny和MABK不等式Bell值退化的影响，发现相干偏差导致周期性的角度窗口且宽度随参与方数增加而缩小，而非相干翻转则给出单一临界翻转概率。连接退化Bell值与Devetak-Winter密钥率边界表明，密钥生成对测量误差的要求比非局域性认证更严格。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多体Bell非局域性是设备无关量子信息协议的核心资源，但实际认证不可避免地受非完美测量的影响。现有研究缺乏对不同类型的测量误差如何影响多体非局域性认证和密钥率估计的系统分析，特别是相干角度偏差与非相干结果翻转在不同Bell不等式下的表现差异尚不明确。

Method: 以n体GHZ态为研究对象，基于Mermin、Svetlichny和MABK三种Bell不等式，分别分析两种测量误差模型：相干角度偏差（coherent angular misalignment）和非相干结果翻转（incoherent outcome flipping）。计算Bell值的退化行为，并将退化后的Bell值与Devetak-Winter密钥率上界进行关联，采用凸组合攻击模型进行分析。

Result: 相干偏差产生周期性的角度违规窗口，窗口宽度随参与方数量n增加而缩小；非相干翻转给出单一临界翻转概率，该概率在MABK和奇数n的Mermin不等式中随n增大而增大，但在Svetlichny不等式中随n增大而减小。密钥生成对测量误差的约束比非局域性认证更严格。

Conclusion: 这些结果提供了在测量误差条件下鲁棒的多体非局域性认证和密钥率估计的定量基准，揭示了不同Bell不等式对不同类型误差的敏感度差异，以及密钥生成对测量精度提出更苛刻的要求。

Abstract: Multipartite Bell nonlocality is a central resource for device-independent quantum information protocols, but its practical certification is inevitably affected by imperfect measurements. We analyze how coherent angular misalignment and incoherent outcome flipping affect Bell-value degradation and nonlocality thresholds in $n$-partite GHZ states based on the Mermin, Svetlichny, and Mermin--Ardehali--Belinskii--Klyshko (MABK) inequalities. Coherent misalignment produces periodic angular violation windows whose individual widths shrink with the number of parties. In contrast, incoherent outcome flipping yields a single critical outcome-flipping probability, which increases with $n$ for MABK and the odd-$n$ Mermin inequalities, but decreases with $n$ for the Svetlichny inequality. Connecting the degraded Bell values to asymptotic Devetak--Winter key-rate bounds under a convex-combination attack model shows that secret-key generation imposes stricter constraints on measurement imperfections than nonlocality certification. These results provide quantitative benchmarks for robust multipartite nonlocality certification and key-rate estimation under measurement imperfections.

</details>


### [99] [Machine learning development for quantum computing and neutrino physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13699)
*Annalisa De Lorenzis*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本论文研究了机器学习方法在量子计算和中微子物理中的应用，重点探讨了量子极限学习机（QELMs）中编码策略、哈密顿量结构与经典可模拟性的关系，以及卷积神经网络在水切伦科夫探测器图像分类中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索机器学习（包括经典和量子形式）作为分析基础物理中复杂高维数据的有效框架，特别关注量子计算与中微子物理两个前沿领域的数据表示与处理问题。

Method: 第一部分采用量子极限学习机（QELMs）混合量子-经典框架，分析编码策略、特征降维、哈密顿量结构及测量对量子动力学、表达性、纠缠和经典可模拟性的影响。第二部分采用卷积架构（包括残差网络）对水切伦科夫探测器产生的模拟图像数据进行分类分析。

Result: 证明了量子极限学习机中量子动力学与表达性及经典可模拟性之间的关键关系；卷积神经网络能够从水切伦科夫探测器数据中有效提取相关信息，实现对复杂事件的准确分类。

Conclusion: 机器学习（经典与量子形式）是分析基础物理中复杂数据的有力框架，同时指出了该领域面临的相关挑战和未来研究方向。

Abstract: This thesis investigates the application of machine-learning methods in the context of quantum computing and neutrino physics, with particular emphasis on the construction of effective representations for complex, high-dimensional data. The first part of the work is devoted to Quantum Extreme Learning Machines (QELMs), a hybrid quantum--classical framework in which classical data are encoded into quantum states and processed through fixed quantum dynamics, while learning is performed by a classical readout layer. Within this framework, we analyze the role of encoding strategies, feature-reduction methods, Hamiltonian structure, and measurement, with particular focus on the relationship between quantum dynamics, expressivity, entanglement, and classical simulability. The second part of the thesis concerns the application of deep learning to the analysis of images produced by water Cherenkov detectors in neutrino physics. Convolutional architectures, including residual networks, are developed for the classification of complex events in realistic simulated datasets, showing that such models can effectively extract relevant information from detector data. Taken together, these results highlight the potential of machine learning, in both its classical and quantum forms, as a powerful framework for the analysis of complex data in fundamental physics, while also outlining relevant challenges and directions for future research.

</details>


### [100] [Towards quantum machine learning for assessing the resilience of post-quantum cryptography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13722)
*Jarosław A. Miszczak*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文探索利用量子生成对抗网络（QGAN）加载哈希数字签名的概率分布到量子计算机内存，作为攻击后量子密码原语的第一步，验证了近期的混合量子-经典方法具备此能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算机的潜在能力推动了适用于抵抗量子攻击的密码协议的发展，但当前有限规模的量子计算机仍可用于发现后量子密码协议中的漏洞和弱点。本文旨在利用QGAN的能力为此目的服务。

Method: 采用量子生成对抗网络（QGAN）架构，将哈希数字签名的概率分布加载到量子计算机内存中。

Result: 实验结果证实，近期的混合量子-经典方法具备加载哈希数字签名概率分布所需的能力。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法可作为工作流程的第一步，为利用量子计算攻击后量子密码原语铺平道路。

Abstract: The potential capabilities of quantum computers motivated the development of cryptographic protocols suitable for securing communication against adversaries with access to large fault-tolerant quantum computers. However, even though current quantum computers are limited in terms of size and precision, they can still be useful for finding loopholes and weaknesses in the post-quantum cryptographic protocols. In this work, we present an attempt to utilize the capabilities of Quantum Generative Adversarial Networks (QGANs), one of the promising architectures used in quantum machine learning, for this purpose. We describe an example application of QGAN architecture for the purpose of loading the probability distribution of the hash-based digital signatures into the memory of a quantum computer. Our results confirm that near-term hybrid quantum-classical methods possess capabilities required for this purpose. The presented approach can be used as a first step in the workflow, enabling the utilization of quantum computing for attacking post-quantum cryptographic primitives.

</details>


### [101] [No finite level of the NPA hierarchy is exact for the doubly-tilted CHSH functional near the critical tilt](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13762)
*Anton Pakhunov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明：对于对称临界贝尔不等式族 $B_s$，任何有限NPA层级都无法在临界点邻域内精确刻画量子最大值，并给出了显式的层级依赖下界和系数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gigena等人发现双倾斜CHSH泛函的量子最大值在临界点附近需要无限增长的NPA层级才能精确，并公开提问是否存在任何有限层级足够精确。本文旨在否定这一可能性。

Method: 采用原始构造（primal construction）方法：在每个层级构造精确可行的矩曲线，利用层级均匀的结构律和层级独立的带符号闭式见证函数 $y^*$，满足 $N_k^T\Gamma(y^*)N_k=u_k u_k^T$。证明在 $k\ge 3$ 时无量子态或光滑量子模型曲线能实现增益方向，因此超调严格存在于NPA切锥的非量子部分。使用计算机辅助严格验证，包括整数精确验证和符号化逐域证明。

Result: 对每个 $k\ge 2$，存在显式有理数 $g_k>0$ 和 $s^*_k>0$，使得 $c_k(s)\ge 4-s+g_k s^2$ 在 $(0,s^*_k]$ 上成立；无条件给出 $a_2>1/39, a_3>1/188, a_4>1/641$。量子值仅以三次方式离开局域界，而每个有限层级在临界点邻域内严格超调。

Conclusion: 回答Gigena等人的开放问题：没有任何有限NPA层级能在临界点的任意邻域内精确刻画该贝尔泛函的量子最大值；超调是本质性的、非量子性的。

Abstract: Gigena, Panwar, Scala, Araujo, Farkas and Chaturvedi [npj Quantum Inf. 11, 82 (2025)] determined the quantum maximum of the doubly-tilted CHSH functionals, observed that the Navascues-Pironio-Acin level needed for exactness grows without evident bound toward the critical tilt, and asked whether any finite level suffices. We answer this in the negative. For the symmetric critical family $B_s=(1-s/2)(\langle A_0\rangle+\langle B_0\rangle)+\mathrm{CHSH}$ we prove: for every NPA level $k\ge 2$ there are an explicit rational $g_k>0$ and an $s^*_k>0$ with $c_k(s)\ge 4-s+g_k s^2$ on $(0,s^*_k]$; since the quantum value leaves the local bound only cubically, every finite level strictly overshoots on an interval: no finite level is exact on any neighbourhood of the critical point. Unconditionally $a_2>1/39$, $a_3>1/188$, $a_4>1/641$. The proof is a primal construction: an exactly feasible moment curve at each level, built from level-uniform structural laws and one level-independent signed witness -- a closed-form class function $y^*$ with $N_k^TΓ(y^*)N_k=u_k u_k^T$ at every level. The mechanism forces the sign: for $k\ge 3$ no quantum state and no smooth curve of quantum models can realize the gain direction, so the overshoot lives strictly in the non-quantum part of the NPA tangent cone. The proof is computer-assisted in the strict sense: finite exact-integer verifications with proven degree bounds are constituent parts of the argument; the chain has been re-verified against independent implementations, including a symbolic per-regime proof of the witness identity and a clean-room implementation written from the paper text alone. The one external input is the published quantum value of Gigena et al., cross-checked to twelve digits.

</details>


### [102] [A phase transition in the exactness of the NPA hierarchy at the critical doubly-tilted CHSH functional](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13774)
*Anton Pakhunov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文量化了双倾斜CHSH泛函的量子最大值与NPA层级收敛行为，揭示了层级精确性在临界线处的相变现象，并证明了超临界侧所有层级的精确性，以及亚临界侧前四个层级的精确性。核心机制是Motzkin多项式作为极限障碍，且边界Hessian的非退化性决定了有限层级可证性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gigena等人证明了双倾斜CHSH泛函的确切量子最大值，但观察到NPA层级所需级别在临界线附近无界增长。本文旨在量化该机制并解释这一现象的根本原因。

Method: 在对称切片s=2-α-β上，分别推导量子值的三次展开、各NPA层级的二次展开及几乎量子系数的精确计算；利用三个显式有理证书证明超临界侧所有层级的精确性；通过Sturm定理和有理伪矩证明亚临界侧前四个层级的精确性；识别极限障碍为Motzkin多项式；利用Nie有限收敛定理和Marshall边界Hessian条件进行几何解读。

Result: (i) 量子值以三次方离开局域界：c_Q=4-s+s^3/6-s^4/36+O(s^5)；(ii) 各NPA层级以二次方超调：c_k(s)=4-s+a_k s^2+O(s^3)，几乎量子系数a_{1+AB}=3/64；(iii) 所需精确层级的发散等价于序列(a_k)的正性；(iv) 超临界侧（α,β≥1）所有层级精确；(v) 亚临界侧前四个层级精确；(vi) 边界Hessian在双倾斜立方接触处退化，导致有限层级不可证。

Conclusion: NPA层级的精确性在临界线处经历相变：超临界侧完全精确，亚临界侧有限层级失效。失效根源是Motzkin多项式的非负非平方和性质，对应边界Hessian退化（接触阶数为三）。同时修正了Gigena等人发表的三个多项式系统错误。

Abstract: Gigena et al. [npj Quantum Inf. 11, 82 (2025)] proved the exact quantum maximum of the doubly-tilted CHSH functional $B_{αβ}=α\langle A_0\rangle+β\langle B_0\rangle+\mathrm{CHSH}$ and observed that the NPA level required to reach it grows without evident bound toward the critical line $α+β=2$. We quantify the mechanism on the symmetric slice $s=2-α-β$: (i) the quantum value leaves the local bound cubically, $c_Q=4-s+s^3/6-s^4/36+O(s^5)$; (ii) each NPA level overshoots quadratically, $c_k(s)=4-s+a_k s^2+O(s^3)$, with the almost-quantum coefficient computed exactly, $a_{1+AB}=3/64$; (iii) the divergence of the required exact level is equivalent to positivity of the single sequence $(a_k)$ - proven for every $k$ in the companion paper. We prove the supercritical side completely: for all $α,β\ge 1$ and every level the hierarchy is exact, via three explicit rational certificates realizing an affine identity. The hierarchy's exactness thus undergoes a phase transition at the critical line. On the subcritical side we certify the first four levels in exact arithmetic (rational pseudo-moments beating $c_Q$, confirmed by Sturm's theorem). We identify the exact mechanism: rescaled to the critical corner, the limiting obstruction is the Motzkin polynomial, the classical nonnegative-but-not-sum-of-squares form, so the finite-level failure sits in the restricted-certificate regime. The phase boundary has a precise geometric reading via Nie's finite-convergence theorem and Marshall's boundary Hessian condition: a self-tested optimum is finitely NPA-certifiable whenever its boundary Hessian is nondegenerate (contact order two), which holds for the single tilt and fails exactly at the doubly-tilted cubic touch. Three verified errata in the published polynomial system of Gigena et al. are documented.

</details>


### [103] [Foliated Quantum Error Correction for Qudits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13784)
*Gözde Üstün,Simon J. Devitt,Jason Saied*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种框架，可将任意基于Pauli的素数维度qudit量子纠错码转化为foliated qudit图态，通过测量该图态即可实现容错测量型量子计算。针对d维toric码、完美[[5,1,3]]码和蜂窝码等例子进行了分析，并数值计算了foliated qudit toric码的阈值，显示其与非foliated版本相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在光子学等平台中，测量型量子计算是一种自然范式，且高维态易于获取。因此，需要一种通用的框架，能够将任意基于Pauli的素数维度qudit量子纠错码转化为适合测量型量子计算的图态结构。

Method: 提出了一种foliation框架，将任意素数维度d的Pauli量子纠错码（包括稳定子码、CSS码、非CSS码以及动力学码）转化为qudit图态。通过分析qudit toric码、完美[[5,1,3]]码和CSS蜂窝码等实例，并在简单误差模型下进行数值模拟计算阈值。

Result: 成功实现了多种qudit码的foliation转化，数值计算表明foliated qudit toric码的阈值与非foliated版本相当。

Conclusion: 该框架为高维测量型量子计算提供了通用方案，在光子学等平台具有应用价值。

Abstract: We present a framework for foliating any Pauli-based quantum error-correcting code over prime-dimensional qudits. For any such code, we obtain a qudit graph state that can be measured to perform fault-tolerant measurement-based quantum computing. Such a paradigm is of interest in platforms such as photonics, where measurement-based protocols are natural and high-dimensional states are readily available. We discuss several examples for arbitrary prime dimension $d$, such as the qudit toric code (stabilizer, CSS), the $d$-dimensional perfect $[[5,1,3]]$ code (stabilizer, non-CSS), and a straightforward $d$-dimensional generalization of the CSS honeycomb code (dynamical, CSS). Under a simple error model, we numerically calculate thresholds for the foliated qudit toric code and demonstrate that they are comparable to the non-foliated version.

</details>


### [104] [Bridging Frustration and Non-Hermiticity via COMPASS: An Adaptive Biorthogonal Neural Quantum State Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13790)
*Lavoisier Wah,Flore K. Kunst,Mohamed Hibat-Allah*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出COMPASS方法，结合双正交自适应递归神经量子态与互补优化策略，实现非厄米多体系统的基态求解。研究表明实值ansatz适用于PT对称哈密顿量，复值ansatz适用于一般非厄米系统，并发现了非厄米阻挫系统中的diabolic环拓扑结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非厄米哈密顿量的量子多体模拟面临挑战，现有方法依赖厄米嵌入或绝热延拓，缺乏直接高效的优化框架。同时，PT对称哈密顿量中复值ansatz可能产生虚假虚能量，而真实非厄米系统又需要复值描述，需要系统的ansatz选择指导原则。

Method: 提出COMPASS方法：结合自适应自回归架构（避免马尔可夫链采样）、双正交变分蒙特卡洛方案、以及能量与方差交替最小化的互补优化策略。对PT对称系统采用实值ansatz约束物理流形，对一般非厄米系统采用复值ansatz捕获复基态性质。

Result: 1) 对PT对称哈密顿量，复值ansatz会自发破缺PT对称性产生虚假虚能量，实值ansatz可避免；2) 对一般非厄米系统，复值ansatz是必需的；3) 在阻挫磁性系统中，能隙阻挫可定量抵御非厄米谱不稳定性，阻挫能隙设置PT对称破缺的临界阈值；4) 复化阻挫耦合产生无厄米类比的diabolic环拓扑结构。

Conclusion: COMPASS框架可实现1D和2D非厄米多体系统的直接研究，物理信息指导的ansatz选择对可靠的非厄米模拟至关重要，并在非厄米阻挫系统中发现了新的谱拓扑结构——diabolic环。

Abstract: In this work, we introduce a complementary optimization method for progressive and adaptive state search (COMPASS) based on biorthogonal adaptive recurrent neural quantum states. Our approach combines an adaptive autoregressive architecture with a biorthogonal variational Monte Carlo scheme as well as a complementary optimization scheme that alternates between energy and variance minimization. This enables the stable convergence to ground-state eigenpairs, while avoiding Markov chain sampling through exact autoregressive generation. We demonstrate that for parity-time(PT)-symmetric Hamiltonians, unconstrained complex ansatze can spontaneously break PT symmetry during optimization, even in the unbroken phase, leading to spurious imaginary energies. Real-valued ansatze, on the other hand, naturally constrain the optimization to the correct physical manifold. Conversely, for generic non-Hermitian (NH) Hamiltonians without symmetry protection and complex spectra, complex ansatze are essential for capturing complex ground-state properties. Our results establish that physically-informed ansatz selection is crucial for reliable NH simulations. By combining adaptive architectures, biorthogonal optimization, and symmetry-aware modeling, this framework enables a direct study of 1D and 2D NH many-body systems without Hermitian embeddings or adiabatic continuation. Applying this framework to systems with frustrated magnetism, we show that gap frustration provides a quantitative shield against NH spectral instability, with the frustration gap setting a critical threshold for PT-symmetry breaking. Also, complexifying the frustration coupling itself generates a new topologically nontrivial network of diabolic level crossings, controlled by the phase of the complex coupling, that has no Hermitian analog. We term this novel spectral topology in NH frustrated systems the diabolic ring.

</details>


### [105] [Markovian evolution from a novel scheme](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13809)
*Fardin Kheirandish,Zahra Iranshahi,Fatemeh Bakhtiari,Adam Moradian*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种计算开放量子多体系统约化密度矩阵的高效框架，通过将环境建模为初级系统的副本并引入单调衰减耦合来确保单向能量流，直接得到满足Lindblad主方程的精确解，避免了昂贵的Liouvillian对角化计算，并在多个典型模型中验证了其准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决玻色子和费米子开放量子多体系统中，由于希尔伯特空间指数增长导致的约化密度矩阵数值求解难题，避免昂贵的Liouvillian对角化计算。

Method: 将环境建模为初级系统的副本，引入单调衰减耦合以强制执行单向能量流，直接从该构造中推导出满足Lindblad主方程的精确解。

Result: 在多个典型模型上的验证表明，该方法能在广泛的参数范围内准确再现耗散动力学行为。

Conclusion: 该工作为模拟马尔可夫开放系统演化提供了一种直接且强大的工具，可立即应用于多体物理中的量子输运和控制问题。

Abstract: The Markovian dynamics of open quantum many-body systems are typically governed by the Lindblad master equation, yet obtaining the reduced density matrix for bosonic and fermionic systems remains a formidable numerical challenge due to the exponential growth of the Hilbert space. Here, we introduce a computationally efficient framework for constructing the reduced density matrix by modeling the environment as a copy of the primary system with a monotonically decaying coupling that enforces unidirectional energy flow. Our method directly yields exact solutions that rigorously satisfy the Lindblad master equation for both bosonic and fermionic cases, bypassing the need for costly Liouvillian diagonalization. We validate our approach through applications to paradigmatic models, demonstrating accurate reproduction of dissipative dynamics across a broad parameter regime. This work provides a straightforward and powerful tool for simulating Markovian open-system evolution, with immediate applicability to quantum transport and control problems in many-body physics.

</details>


### [106] [Quantum Algorithm for Elliptic Curve Discrete Logarithms with Space-Efficient Point Addition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13816)
*Han Luo,Ziyi Yang,Jingquan Luo,Ziruo Wang,Yuexin Su,Xiaoming Sun,Lvzhou Li,Tongyang Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种针对素数域椭圆曲线离散对数问题（ECDLP）的空间高效量子算法，仅需3n+6⌊log₂n⌋+O(1)逻辑量子比特和919n³/log₂n+O(n²)托福利门。对于256位素数域曲线，仅需835个逻辑量子比特，优于此前Chevignard等人（1098比特）和Babbush等人（1175比特）的估计。关键创新在于一种新的空间高效可逆模逆电路，通过扩展欧几里得算法（EEA）改进Proos-Zalka的寄存器共享技术，引入长度寄存器和位置控制算术，仅用2n+6⌊log₂n⌋+O(1)量子比特和195n²+O(n log₂n)托福利门实现模逆运算，结合中间测量和经典前馈操作，实现了Shor算法在ECDLP上的完整空间高效实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 减少求解ECDLP的量子算法资源需求是重要目标，特别是逻辑量子比特数量，因为量子硬件的量子比特资源极其有限且昂贵。

Method: 提出一种新的空间高效可逆模逆电路：基于扩展欧几里得算法（EEA），改进Proos-Zalka的寄存器共享技术，引入长度寄存器和位置控制算术来紧凑存储和更新中间变量，并优化可逆更新过程及相应的受控算术电路。

Result: 模逆电路仅需2n+6⌊log₂n⌋+O(1)量子比特和195n²+O(n log₂n)托福利门；完整ECDLP量子算法仅需3n+6⌊log₂n⌋+O(1)量子比特和919n³/log₂n+O(n²)托福利门。对于256位曲线，仅需835逻辑量子比特，较此前最优结果减少约24%-29%。

Conclusion: 新的空间高效模逆电路显著降低了ECDLP量子算法的量子比特需求，使Shor算法在近量子硬件上的实现更加可行，为后量子密码安全性评估提供了更精确的资源估计。

Abstract: The Elliptic Curve Discrete Logarithm Problem (ECDLP) is a fundamental problem in cryptography, and reducing the resource requirements of quantum algorithms for solving ECDLP is an important goal. In this work, we present a space-efficient quantum algorithm for solving the ECDLP over prime fields, achieving an implementation with only $3n+6\lfloor \log_2 n \rfloor+O(1)$ logical qubits and $919n^3/\log_2 n+O(n^2)$ Toffoli gates, where $n$ is the bit-length of the prime. For a 256-bit prime-field curve, our construction requires only 835 logical qubits, reducing the previous best estimates of 1098 and 1175 logical qubits by Chevignard et al. [EUROCRYPT 2026] and Babbush et al. [ArXiv Preprint 2026], respectively.
  The key to our improvement is a new space-efficient reversible modular inversion circuit, which addresses the dominant space bottleneck in affine-coordinate point addition. Starting from the extended Euclidean algorithm (EEA), we refine the register-sharing technique of Proos and Zalka by introducing length registers and location-controlled arithmetic to compactly store and update intermediate variables. We further optimize the reversible update procedures and construct the corresponding controlled arithmetic circuits, resulting in a modular inversion circuit implemented by only $2n+6\lfloor \log_2 n \rfloor+O(1)$ logical qubits and $195n^2+O(n\log_2 n)$ Toffoli gates. This modular inversion circuit together with mid-circuit measurements and classical feed-forward operations provides a space-efficient controlled affine point-addition circuit and a complete implementation of Shor's algorithm for ECDLP.

</details>


### [107] [Inherent interpretability provides inherent value in quantum machine learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13827)
*Kaitlin Gili,Zachary P. Bradshaw*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文主张量子机器学习（QML）的价值不应仅通过任务性能来评判，而应关注其内在可解释性——即量子模型的数学结构如何为特定ML任务提供有意义的行为贡献。通过量子傅里叶模型与随机傅里叶特征在高斯过程核逼近中的对比，以及量子信息工具（对称性、度量几何、拓扑）带来的丰富归纳偏置，论证量子模型的内在可解释性可能成为其在实际ML应用中落地的关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典机器学习领域正面临不可解释神经网络在实际部署中的困境，模型可解释性对领域适配和人类采用至关重要。作者将这一视角引入QML，认为QML社区过度关注与经典大规模神经网络直接竞争的性能指标，而忽略了量子模型自身固有的数学结构所赋予的可解释性价值。

Method: 通过一个具体动机性示例（量子傅里叶模型与RFF在GP核近似中的对比）展示两种数学构造的自上而下与自下而上的互补性，并综述QML文献中利用量子信息工具（对称性、度量几何、拓扑）设计内在可解释模型的案例。

Result: 量子傅里叶模型相比RFF在高斯过程核设计与不确定性量化任务中提供了不同的工具集和可解释性发现途径；量子信息工具能够引入丰富的归纳偏置，用于设计针对特定任务的固有可解释ML模型。

Conclusion: 呼吁QML社区将量子模型的内在组件和机制与任务性能分开评估，因为内在可解释性可能成为量子模型（乃至量子计算机）在实际ML中得到采用的根本原因。

Abstract: The field of quantum machine learning (QML) evolved to value models believed to most directly rival those providing utility in classical ML, namely large-scale neural networks. Although more recently, classical ML has been learning a hard lesson with respect to deploying un-interpretable neural networks in the wild: model interpretability matters for domain-adapted co-design and human adoption. We adopt this larger ML perspective to argue that quantum ML model value can be found through the characterization of its inherent interpretability offerings -- i.e. its mathematical structure that contributes meaningfully to desired model behavior for the specific ML task. To support our perspective, we provide a motivating example of a characterization with quantum Fourier models and random Fourier features (RFF) as approaches to approximate Gaussian process (GP) kernels for uncertainty quantification tasks in ML. The top-down and bottom-up complementarity of the two mathematical constructions reveals that quantum Fourier models offer different tools than RFFs for principled GP kernel design and interpretable discovery for uncertainty quantification with real-world data. To showcase the rich variety of inductive biases enabled by quantum information tools, we review examples from the QML literature -- including symmetry, metric geometry, and topology -- that can be used to design inherently interpretable ML models for specific tasks. We hope this framing encourages the QML community to value the inherent components and mechanisms of quantum models separately from task performance, as inherent interpretability might be the reason that a quantum model, and potentially a quantum computer, gets used in practice for ML.

</details>


### [108] [Post-Critical Meson Dynamics of Kibble-Zurek Excitations in a 5,564-Qubit Quantum Annealer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13842)
*Francis A. Bayocboc,Jacek Dziarmaga,Marek M. Rams,Jaka Vodeb*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用大规模量子退火器（含数千超导量子比特）研究纵向偏置量子伊辛链中Kibble-Zurek缺陷生成后的非可积后临界动力学，发现缺陷密度符合理论预言，但磁化、空间分布等揭示介子演化被后临界局域化中断，表明量子退火器可探测临界激发在缺陷计数之外的命运。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子相变后临界动力学与初始缺陷生成同等重要，但此前缺乏在可编程大规模量子系统上研究非可积后临界演化（特别是缺陷被束缚成介子态后的动力学）的实验能力。

Method: 使用量子退火器实现纵向偏置量子伊辛链，结合能量尺度重标定和零噪声外推法测量缺陷密度，并与矩阵乘积态（MPS）模拟对比；通过磁化、空间分布和少数域统计研究后临界动力学。

Result: 缺陷密度遵循偏置Kibble-Zurek/Landau-Zener交叉并和均匀偏置MPS模拟一致；但磁化、空间分布和少数域统计显示介子演化被后临界域图案的局域化中断。含无序的MPS模拟能重现这种缺陷生成与后临界局域化动力学的分离。

Conclusion: 大规模量子退火器能够探测超出缺陷计数的临界激发命运，揭示了非可积后临界动力学中缺陷生成与局域化介子演化的分离。

Abstract: Quantum phase transitions provide a controlled route for generating many-body excitations, but the dynamics after the critical point can be as important as the initial defect creation. Recent progress in quantum annealing has made it possible to access coherent nonequilibrium dynamics in programmable Ising systems with thousands of superconducting qubits. Here we use this capability to study a longitudinally biased quantum Ising chain, where Kibble--Zurek defect creation is followed by nonintegrable post-critical dynamics. The longitudinal bias confines kink--antikink excitations into mesonic bound states, so that the final spin configurations encode both the production of defects near the critical point and the subsequent evolution of the confined excitations. Using energy-scale rescaling and zero-noise extrapolation, we find that the defect density follows the expected biased Kibble--Zurek/Landau--Zener crossover and agrees with matrix-product-state simulations with uniform bias. In contrast, magnetization, spatial profiles, and minority-domain statistics reveal that mesonic evolution is interrupted by localization of the post-critical domain pattern. Matrix-product-state simulations with disorder reproduce this separation between robust defect creation and localized post-critical dynamics. Our results show that large-scale quantum annealers can probe the fate of critical excitations beyond defect counting.

</details>


### [109] [Quantum Topological Data Encoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13847)
*Adam Wesołowski,Dimitrios Thanos,Daniel Leykam,Lirandë Pira*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出量子拓扑数据编码(QTDE)框架，通过拓扑驱动量子演化将高维数据的拓扑信息编码到量子态中，在团复形分类任务上优于基于组合拉普拉斯算子的基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多领域的数据具有丰富的几何和拓扑结构，传统向量表示难以捕捉；量子机器学习虽能处理高维希尔伯特空间数据，但性能取决于经典数据到量子态的编码方式。

Method: 提出量子拓扑数据编码(QTDE)框架，将现有拓扑驱动量子编码方法推广到高维数据，通过拓扑驱动量子演化将拓扑信息编码到量子态中。

Result: 在团复形分类任务上，QTDE方法持续优于基于组合拉普拉斯算子直接比较的基线方法，初步证明拓扑驱动量子表示能捕捉超越经典拓扑描述符的判别信息。

Conclusion: QTDE框架可提供更高效、更可靠的数据表示，并在多个应用领域具有潜力。

Abstract: Many datasets encountered across a wide range of domains possess rich geometric and topological structure that is difficult to capture using conventional vector-based representations. Quantum machine learning offers the possibility of processing high-dimensional data in Hilbert spaces, but its practical success depends critically on how classical data is encoded into quantum states. We introduce \emph{quantum topological data encoding} (QTDE), a general framework for encoding topological information into quantum states via topology-driven quantum evolution. Our method generalises an existing topology-driven quantum encoding framework to higher-dimensional data. We test the proposed method on clique-complexes classification tasks, and provide preliminary evidence that topology-driven quantum representations can capture discriminative information beyond that available through direct comparisons of classical topological descriptors. The proposed quantum representations consistently outperform a baseline based on direct comparisons of the combinatorial Laplacians describing the underlying topological structure. We indicate several areas of application where the framework can be used to provide a more efficient and reliable data representation.

</details>


### [110] [A Lie-algebraic approach to non-Markovian quantum dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13865)
*Haijin Ding,Stephen S. -T. Yau,Zhiwen Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文从李代数视角出发，利用Magnus展开研究非马尔可夫量子动力学及其截断误差，并扩展到随机滤波情形，通过数值模拟验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非马尔可夫量子动力学在量子计算中普遍存在，但其数值模拟面临挑战；现有方法对非马尔可夫性如何影响数值精度的理解尚不充分，亟需从李代数角度进行系统性分析。

Method: 将密度矩阵矢量化，将非马尔可夫演化表示为高维线性时变方程；采用Magnus展开进行近似，分析一阶和二阶截断误差与非马尔可夫性之间的关系；对于含测量噪声的随机滤波情形，分别用Itô和Stratonovich两种方式建模，并研究相应Magnus展开的差异。

Result: 揭示了非马尔可夫特性对Magnus展开截断误差的影响规律；发现量子测量噪声的建模方式（Itô vs Stratonovich）会导致不同的Magnus展开形式及截断误差；数值模拟验证了Magnus展开在非马尔可夫量子动力学（含或不含随机性）模拟中的有效性。

Conclusion: Magnus展开是一种模拟非马尔可夫量子动力学的有效数值工具；截断误差受非马尔可夫性及噪声建模方式共同影响；李代数结构在Liouville空间中起着关键作用。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the non-Markovian quantum dynamics in quantum computations from the perspective of a Lie algebraic approach based on numerical analysis. By vectorizing the density matrix of quantum states, the non-Markovian evolutions can be represented with high-dimensional linear time-varying equations, where the time-varying parameters arise from the non-Markovian interactions between the quantum system and environment. We study the Magnus expansion of such linear time-varying quantum dynamics and clarify how the truncation errors for the first- and second-order Magnus expansions are influenced by the non-Markovian properties. Besides, when the quantum states are measured for filtering, the dynamics can be modeled as time-varying stochastic differential equations due to the existence of measurement noise. The Magnus expansions based on quantum stochastic filtering are different when the quantum measurement noises are modeled in an {Itô} or Stratonovich approach, rendering different truncation errors. Based on this, numerical simulations further demonstrate the efficiency of Magnus expansions in simulating non-Markovian quantum dynamics without or with stochasticity, and how the truncation errors are influenced by the Lie algebras in the Liouville space.

</details>


### [111] [Continuous limit of the square well problem in quantum mechanics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13872)
*Matheus D. Moro,Thiago T. Tsutsui,Antonio S. M. de Castro,Fabiano M. Andrade*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过傅里叶变换系统展示了当方势阱宽度趋于无穷时，自由粒子解如何从方势阱问题中涌现出来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子力学中自由粒子和方势阱是两个标志性问题，分别展示了物质波、能量量子化和束缚态等核心概念。自然地，需要考虑方势阱宽度趋于无穷时，其解如何过渡到自由粒子解。

Method: 对方势阱的波动方程应用傅里叶变换，通过系统的数学推导来展示从束缚态到自由粒子解的过渡过程。

Result: 建立了一个系统的程序，成功演示了当方势阱宽度趋于无穷时，方势阱解如何平滑过渡到自由粒子解。

Conclusion: 该工作为量子力学教学中理解自由粒子与方势阱问题之间的内在联系提供了清晰的数学框架。

Abstract: The free-particle and square-well potentials are two of the most emblematic problems in quantum mechanics, illustrating essential concepts such as matter waves, energy quantization, and bound states. It is therefore natural to consider how the free-particle solutions emerge from the square well as the width approaches infinity. In this work, we present a systematic procedure to demonstrate this transition by applying a Fourier transform to the wave equation.

</details>


### [112] [A Reservoir Computing Approach to Quantum Gate Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13887)
*Francesco Caravelli,Roberto Menta,Antonio Sannia*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出基于储层计算（Group Reservoir Computing）的量子门合成方法，利用Wei--Norman分解保证演化酉性并给出误差界。在单量子比特标准门集上，单次前向传播平均保真度达0.94，用于预热梯度优化可减少约半数迭代次数，且适用于任意有限维硬件平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子门合成方法计算开销大，亟需更高效的方案以在实际硬件上实现量子算法。

Method: 提出Group Reservoir Computing方法，基于Wei--Norman分解实现紧凑演化描述；训练仅需单次线性回归，且通过构造保证重建动力学始终酉性，并推导了形式误差界。

Result: 在单量子比特标准门集上，单次前向传播平均保真度0.94；用于预热梯度优化时，达到目标保真度所需迭代次数约为纯梯度上升法的一半。

Conclusion: 该方法通用性强，适用于任意有限维硬件平台，并讨论了向多量子比特合成的扩展路径。

Abstract: Quantum gate synthesis is essential for implementing quantum algorithms on real hardware, yet existing methods are often computationally demanding. Here, we introduce a novel approach based on reservoir computing, which we name Group Reservoir Computing, an efficient machine-learning paradigm for learning temporal dynamics whose training reduces to a single linear regression, to reduce the resources required. The method is grounded in the Wei--Norman decomposition, which provides a compact description of the evolution. We prove that the reconstructed dynamics always remain unitary by construction and derive formal error bounds that establish the theoretical validity of the strategy. On the standard single-qubit gate set the trained network produces a control pulse in a single pass, with mean fidelity 0.94 across the eight benchmark gates; used to warm-start gradient-based optimization, it roughly halves the number of iterations that plain gradient ascent needs to reach a target fidelity, so that the relevant figure of merit is the time to reach that threshold rather than the final accuracy after a fixed budget. Owing to its general formulation, the method applies to any finite-dimensional hardware platform; the route to multiqubit synthesis is discussed in the closing section.

</details>


### [113] [Quantum observables for probabilistic classical particles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13937)
*Christof Wetterich*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出使用更稳健的量子统计观测量来描述微观粒子，从经典统计力学中的刘维尔方程出发，展示了量子系统如何从经典统计学中涌现，并具体以谐振子和库仑势为例验证了离散能谱和双缝干涉等量子特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的位置和动量观测量对于具有显著色散的微观概率分布并不适用，因此需要寻找更合适的统计观测量来描述微观粒子。

Method: 采用量子形式主义处理经典统计的刘维尔方程，引入非对易的统计观测量算符，并在一般势能下推导量子系统的涌现，特别针对谐振子和库仑势进行了子系统分析。

Result: 成功展示了氢原子的离散能谱和量子谐振子的能谱，以及双缝干涉实验的干涉现象；同时指出贝尔不等式不适用于这些统计观测量。

Conclusion: 经典统计学可以涌现出量子力学特征，统计观测量具有非对易性，这种方法不仅适用于微观粒子，也可能对尘埃或行星的概率动力学有参考意义。

Abstract: The classical observables of position and momentum are not well adapted to particles in a microphysical situation where typical probability distributions are characterized by a substantial dispersion. We propose the use of more robust quantum observables for probabilistic classical particles. The quantum observables are statistical observables which do not take fixed values for a given classical position and momentum. Solutions of the Liouville equation are discussed in the quantum formalism for classical statistics. Statistical observables are represented by non-commuting operators. No classical correlation function is defined for these observables and Bell's inequalities do not apply. We demonstrate for a general potential how a quantum system emerges from classical statistics. For the particular cases of a harmonic potential and a Coulomb potential we investigate subsystems which describe all features of a quantum particle. This covers the discrete energy spectrum of the hydrogen atom and quantum harmonic oscillator. We discuss the interference for the double-slit experiment. Conserved statistical observables may also be relevant for the probabilistic dynamics of dust or planets.

</details>


### [114] [Universal Quantum Computation with Multi-Mode Schrödinger Cat States Stabilized by Non-Local Dissipation Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13975)
*Jesper Lind-Olsen,Jonas Lidal,Tron Omland,Joakim Bergli*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于耗散稳定的多模薛定谔猫态的通用量子计算架构，通过引入通用量子门集（包括任意X轴旋转、Z轴π/2旋转和XX(π/2)纠缠门），实现了对耗散稳定猫量子比特的通用量子计算。数值模拟验证了在 realistic 参数下的高保真门动力学和纠缠生成能力，并分析了光子损失、无序等因素的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前已有工作证明了多模薛定谔猫态的耗散稳定化可作为鲁棒的量子存储器，但缺乏通用量子计算的框架。本文旨在填补这一空白，为耗散稳定的多模猫量子比特建立通用量子计算架构。

Method: 采用 Kerr 非线性振子链，通过工程化的非局域耗散和有效的低维描述，实现任意单量子比特控制（绕X轴的任意旋转和绕Z轴的π/2旋转）。通过耦合两个稳定化阵列（各自仅通过一个振子连接），实现XX(π/2)门的相干纠缠操作。使用数值模拟验证方案有效性。

Result: 数值模拟表明，在 realistic 参数下，该方案可实现高保真度的门动力学和纠缠生成；分析了诱导和本征光子损失、无序以及有效低维理论的 validity 范围。

Conclusion: 耗散稳定的多模薛定谔猫态有潜力成为通用玻色量子计算的架构基础。

Abstract: Schrödinger cat states provide a hardware-efficient platform for bosonic quantum error correction by encoding logical information in protected manifolds of harmonic oscillators. While previous work has demonstrated the dissipative stabilization of multi-mode Schrödinger cat states as robust quantum memories, a framework for universal quantum computation has remained unavailable. Here we extend this approach by introducing a universal gate set for dissipatively stabilized multi-mode cat qubits. Using a chain of Kerr non-linear oscillators coupled through engineered non-local dissipation and an effective low-dimensional description, we show how arbitrary single-qubit control can be achieved through arbitrary rotation around the $X$-axis and $π/2$-rotation around the $Z$-axis. We further show how coupling two such stabilized arrays through just one oscillator on each respective array enables coherent entangling operations through implementation of the $XX(π/2)$ gate. Numerical simulations demonstrate high-fidelity gate dynamics and entanglement generation under realistic parameters. Finally, we analyze the effects of induced and intrinsic photon loss, disorder, and the validity regime of the effective low-dimensional theory. Our results establish dissipatively stabilized multi-mode Schrödinger cat states as a potential architecture for universal bosonic quantum computation.

</details>


### [115] [Nonreciprocal Relaxation Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13989)
*Xingyu Zhang,Yihan Ma,Yue Liu,Niaz Ali Khan,Chenlong Huang,Yuguo Su,Junyan Luo,Dahai He*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文发现瞬态工程非互易耗散通道可以为两模系统提供捷径，加速收敛到目标互易非平衡稳态，且该加速效应与方向无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子Mpemba效应的最新发现激发了人们对开放量子系统反常弛豫动力学的兴趣，但如何动态加速向非平衡稳态的收敛仍是一个挑战。

Method: 在相互作用玻色子模式的两模系统中，瞬态激活一个非互易耗散通道，通过抑制模间能量振荡并实现单向热沉排放来加速弛豫。

Result: 非互易通道的瞬态激活能有效抑制长时间模间能量振荡，实现快速单向热排放；该弛豫加速效应稳健且与互易性方向无关。

Conclusion: 该结果为连续变量量子系统中的快速态制备和冷却提供了强大的热力学技术，对低温量子信息处理具有重要意义。

Abstract: Driven by recent discoveries regarding the quantum Mpemba effect, the anomalous relaxation dynamics of open quantum systems have garnered significant attention. While expediting thermalization to equilibrium has been extensively studied, dynamically accelerating the convergence toward nonequilibrium steady states remains a formidable challenge. In this article, we find a transient engineered nonreciprocal dissipative channel can provide a shortcut that accelerates convergence to the target reciprocal nonequilibrium steady state for the considered two-mode model and initial states. Using interacting bosonic modes, we demonstrate that the temporal activation of a nonreciprocal channel efficiently suppresses prolonged inter-mode energy oscillations, enforcing a rapid, unidirectional thermal dump into the environment. Counterintuitively, we find that this relaxation speedup is robust and independent of the direction of the nonreciprocity. Our results provide a powerful thermodynamic technique for rapid state preparation and cooling in continuous-variable quantum systems, particularly critical for low-temperature quantum information processing.

</details>


### [116] [Reshaping quantum annealing landscapes with diagonal catalysts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14063)
*Andrés N. Cáliz,Carlos Ramon-Escandell,Finnley Paolella,Josep Bosch,Jan Nogué,Arnau Riera,Jordi Riu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出ZZ-catalyst方法，通过最小化远离解的状态的竞争性，解决量子退火中粒子被困在局部最小值的问题，在稀疏和全连接模型上均能提升近解概率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子退火常因粒子被困在与解相距多个自旋翻转的局部最小值中而受限，需要一种方法来帮助系统逃离这些局部陷阱。

Method: 建立理解能量与汉明距离关系的数学框架，利用无小挫折子问题的基态模式构建ZZ-catalyst，使远离解的状态在能量上竞争力降低。

Result: 在稀疏问题上，ZZ-catalyst在短扫描下倍增了近解概率；在全连接模型上效果持续，且可通过子问题选择进行调节。

Conclusion: ZZ-catalyst是一种有效的量子退火增强方法，能够提升近解概率，且具有可调性。

Abstract: Quantum annealing is often limited by population trapped in local minima many spin flips from the solution. We introduce a mathematical framework to understand the connection between energy and Hamming distance in optimization problems. Using this, we build ZZ-catalysts from ground-state patterns of small frustration-free subproblems that make configurations far from the solution less energetically competitive. On sparse problems they multiply the near-solution probability at short sweeps, with gains persisting on fully-connected models and tunable via subproblem choice.

</details>


### [117] [Building Shor's Algorithm in Lean: An Agentic Formalization of Quantum Attacks on RSA-2048 and P-256](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14082)
*Lei Zhang,Yusheng Zhao,Hongshun Yao,Xin Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过智能体形式化方法，在Lean定理证明器中形式化了Shor算法家族及其对RSA-2048和椭圆曲线P-256的量子攻击分析，提供了逻辑资源估计，为机器检验的量子密码分析奠定了基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型和智能体系统在形式化定理证明方面展现出潜力，而量子计算中的Shor算法及其变体对Lean形式化提出了高难度挑战。作者希望通过智能体形式化（agentic formalization）方法，将这一算法家族在Lean中形式化，以验证量子密码攻击的数学基础。

Method: 采用智能体形式化方法：软件智能体分析文献、编写Lean代码并修复证明，人类审查科学主张，机器检验形式化证明。形式化范围涵盖从量子求阶算法到可逆量子电路（模运算和椭圆曲线算术），基于[Quantum 5, 433]和[ASIACRYPT 2017, 241–270]分别形式化RSA-2048和P-256的逻辑资源估计，并提供经典操作的额外估计。

Result: 成功在Lean中形式化了Shor算法家族，完成了对RSA-2048（2048位模数）和P-256（256位素数域上的标准椭圆曲线）两种密码学设置中量子攻击的分析，并提供了相应的逻辑资源估计。

Conclusion: 该工作为更广泛的机器检验量子密码分析铺平了道路，代表了向AI辅助设计和验证量子算法迈出的重要一步。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly assisting with demanding formal theorem-proving tasks, particularly when grounded in machine-checked libraries such as Lean. Agentic systems further amplify this process by searching, reusing, and extending existing formal developments to uncover new discoveries. In quantum computing, Shor's algorithm and its variants present such a demanding case for Lean formalization. In this work, we formalize this algorithm family in Lean through agentic formalization: software agents analyze sources, write Lean code and repair proofs, with human review of the scientific claims and machine checking of the resulting formal proofs. Our formalization develops the mathematical foundations for analyzing quantum attacks in two cryptographic settings: a 2048-bit modulus in the RSA-2048 and the standardized elliptic curve over a 256-bit prime field (P-256). To support these analyses, the formalization ranges from quantum algorithms for order finding to reversible quantum circuits for modular and elliptic-curve arithmetic. Based on [Quantum 5, 433] and [ASIACRYPT 2017, 241--270], we formalize the logical resource estimates for RSA-2048 and P-256, respectively, and provide additional estimates of classical operations. We expect the results pave the way for broader machine-checked quantum cryptanalysis and represent a step toward AI-assisted design and verification of quantum algorithms.

</details>


### [118] [Benchmarking trigonometric continuous-variable gate primitives with trapped ions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14085)
*Tommaso Rainaldi,Jake Montgomery,Christopher G. Yale,Brian K. McFarland,Melissa C. Revelle,Daniel Lobser,Edward C. Tortorici,Susan Clark,George Siopsis,Matt Grau,Felix Ringer*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在QSCOUT trapped-ion量子平台上实验演示并基准测试了用于混合连续-离散变量量子处理器的三角函数连续变量（CV）门（cosine gates），通过集体运动模式实现单qumode和多qumode操作，测量了Fock空间跃迁概率并研究了参数依赖性和Trotter步数效应，建立了这些非高斯门作为玻色子哈密顿量模拟和混合量子算法的可重用构建块。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合连续-离散变量量子处理器能够在振荡器模式（qumode）中直接表示玻色自由度，同时使用量子比特进行控制、读取和非线性操作。最近提出的三角函数CV门将振荡器正交量的周期函数提升为基本操作，使其成为紧凑变量、转子模型、晶格规范理论和非谐动力学的自然原语。然而，这些门在实验层面的表征和基准测试尚不充分，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 在QSCOUT trapped-ion量子平台上，利用三离子和四离子$^{171}{\rm Yb}^{+}$链的集体运动模式实现单qumode余弦门，并通过混合qubit-qumode操作和条件相空间位移实现有限步三角函数门电路。测量Fock空间跃迁概率，研究其对门参数和Trotter步数的依赖性，并与包含热初始化和运动退相位的模拟进行比较。同时推导了理想门矩阵元和相空间诊断，将测量结果与这些门生成的非高斯结构联系起来。

Result: 成功实验演示和基准测试了单qumode余弦门，并进行了双qumode余弦门实现的分辨率边缘基准测试。获得了Fock空间跃迁概率的实验数据，揭示了其与门参数和Trotter步数的依赖关系，实验与包含热初始化和运动退相位的模拟结果吻合良好。

Conclusion: 三角函数CV门被确立为玻色子哈密顿量模拟和需要本质非多项式操作的混合量子算法的可重用构建块，为未来在紧凑变量、转子模型、晶格规范理论和非谐动力学等领域的应用提供了实验基础。

Abstract: Hybrid continuous-discrete-variable quantum processors can represent bosonic degrees of freedom directly in oscillator modes, or qumodes, while using qubits for control, readout, and nonlinear operations. Recently proposed trigonometric continuous-variable (CV) gate sets promote periodic functions of oscillator quadratures to elementary operations, making them natural primitives for compact variables, rotor models, lattice gauge theories, and anharmonic dynamics. Here we experimentally demonstrate and benchmark one-qumode cosine gates, and perform a mode-resolved marginal benchmark of two-qumode cosine-gate implementations, on the QSCOUT trapped-ion quantum platform. Our implementation uses collective motional modes of three- and four-ion $^{171}{\rm Yb}^{+}$ chains and realizes finite-step trigonometric-gate circuits through hybrid qubit-qumode operations and conditional phase-space displacements. In contrast to previous theoretical and compilation work, we focus on the gate-level characterization of the trigonometric primitives. We measure Fock-space transition probabilities, study their dependence on gate parameters and Trotter step number, and compare with simulations incorporating thermal initialization and motional dephasing. We also derive ideal gate matrix elements and phase-space diagnostics, connecting the measurements to the non-Gaussian structure generated by these gates. These results establish trigonometric CV gates as reusable building blocks for bosonic Hamiltonian simulations and hybrid quantum algorithms requiring intrinsically non-polynomial operations.

</details>


### [119] [Pair-Partition Constructions for CPM-Based Quantum LDPC Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14091)
*Koki Okada,Kenta Kasai*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用循环置换矩阵（CPMs）构造二元CSS量子LDPC码，通过配对分区方程实现CSS正交性，给出了多个有限实例及其参数（如[[372,130,16]]等），并通过穷举低权重排除和非稳定子见证者确认了距离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构造具有良好参数（高码率、大距离）的二元CSS量子LDPC码，克服量子纠错码构造中的挑战。

Method: 基于循环置换矩阵（CPMs）和配对分区的J×J阵列，通过线性配对差分方程施加CSS正交条件；参数由列重J、行重L和素数提升大小P控制。

Result: 给出了多个有限实例：(J,L)=(4,12)的[[372,130,16]]码（码率0.349），(J,L)=(4,14)的[[518,228,16]]码（码率0.440），以及(J,L)=(3,8)的[[472,122,14]]和[[488,126,14]]码；距离通过穷举低权重排除和非稳定子见证者确认。

Conclusion: 成功构造了具有CSS正交性的量子LDPC码实例，展示了基于CPM构造方法的有效性。

Abstract: We construct binary Calderbank--Shor--Steane (CSS) quantum low-density parity-check (LDPC) codes from circulant permutation matrices (CPMs). The construction is parameterized by column weight J, row weight L, and prime lift size P. A J x J array of pair partitions imposes linear paired-difference equations on the CPM exponents. These equations give CSS orthogonality. The main finite examples reported here are the (J,L)=(4,12)-regular girth-six code [[372,130,16]] with rate 0.349, and the (J,L)=(4,14)-regular girth-six code [[518,228,16]] with rate 0.440. We also record (J,L)=(3,8)-regular girth-six instances [[472,122,14]] and [[488,126,14]], with lift sizes P=59 and P=61, respectively. The stated distances are established for the fixed matrices by exhaustive low-weight exclusion together with explicit non-stabilizer witnesses.

</details>

# math.SG [[Back]](#toc)

### [120] [The Lagrangian flux-monodromy morphism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13900)
*Joé Brendel,Jean-Philippe Chassé,Rémi Leclercq*

Main category: math.SG

TL;DR: 本文在Lagrangian子流形空间的基本群上定义了一个新的态射，该态射考虑了Lagrangian通量和单值性，研究了其像的离散性及其与Lagrangian Hamilton轨道C∞拓扑的关系，并在ℝ⁴和ℂP²中完全描述了Lagrangian环面的新态射，还将ℝ⁴中的结果从C∞拓扑升级到Hausdorff拓扑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Lagrangian子流形空间中，已有对基本群及其元素（如Lagrangian通量）的研究，但尚未有将通量与单值性结合起来考虑的态射。本文旨在填补这一空白，定义一个新的态射并研究其性质。

Method: 在Lagrangian子流形空间的基本群上定义了一个考虑Lagrangian通量和单值性的新态射；通过分析该态射像的离散性，将其与Lagrangian的Hamilton轨道的C∞拓扑联系起来；对ℝ⁴和ℂP²中的Lagrangian环面进行了完全描述并给出显式构造；利用类似构造研究Clifford环面的形状不变量；最后将ℝ⁴中的结果从C∞拓扑升级到Hausdorff拓扑。

Result: 完全描述了ℝ⁴和ℂP²中Lagrangian环面的新态射；给出了显式构造；描述了Clifford环面在ℂPⁿ中的形状不变量；将ℝ⁴中Lagrangian环面的结果从C∞拓扑提升到Hausdorff拓扑。

Conclusion: 该新态射是研究Lagrangian子流形空间拓扑性质的有效工具，其像的离散性与Hamilton轨道的拓扑紧密相关，且相关结果在不同拓扑（C∞和Hausdorff）下均可成立。

Abstract: We define a new morphism on the fundamental group of the space of Lagrangian submanifolds, which takes into account the Lagrangian flux and monodromy. We study the discreteness of its image and relate this discreteness to the ($C^\infty$) topology of the Hamiltonian orbit of the Lagrangian in question. We completely describe this new morphism for Lagrangian tori in $\mathbb{R}^4$ and $\mathbb{C}P^2$ and give explicit constructions. Similar constructions allow us to study the shape invariant of the Clifford torus in $\mathbb{C}P^n$. Finally, we upgrade our results on Lagrangian tori in $\mathbb{R}^4$ from the $C^\infty$ topology to the Hausdorff one.

</details>


### [121] [The spread construction and non-uniqueness of visible Lagrangians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14010)
*Joé Brendel,Reto Kaufmann*

Main category: math.SG

TL;DR: 本文证明每个Hirzebruch曲面都同时包含一个拉格朗日环面和拉格朗日Klein瓶，它们都能在矩映射下满射到整个矩多面体。这一发现表明可见拉格朗日子流形的拓扑不由其矩映射像决定。证明基于称为“Hamilton微分同胚族扩散”的新构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前期已知偶Hirzebruch曲面包含拉格朗日环面、奇Hirzebruch曲面包含拉格朗日Klein瓶作为其实轨迹。作者想探究是否每个Hirzebruch曲面都能同时包含这两种类型的拉格朗日子流形，并研究可见拉格朗日子流形的拓扑性质与矩映射像之间的关系。

Method: 提出了一种称为“Hamilton微分同胚族扩散”（spread of a family of Hamiltonian diffeomorphisms）的新构造方法，利用该方法在Hirzebruch曲面上构造所需的拉格朗日子流形。

Result: 证明每个Hirzebruch曲面（无论偶或奇）都同时包含一个拉格朗日环面和一个拉格朗日Klein瓶，且它们都能在矩映射下满射到整个矩多面体。由此得出可见拉格朗日子流形的拓扑不由其矩映射像唯一决定的结论。

Conclusion: Hirzebruch曲面在可见拉格朗日子流形方面具有更丰富的结构，传统的通过矩映射像推断拓扑的方法在此失效，需要更精细的不变量来区分可见拉格朗日子流形。

Abstract: Every even symplectic Hirzebruch surface contains a Lagrangian torus and every odd symplectic Hirzebruch surface contains a Lagrangian Klein bottle as their respective real locus. From a toric point of view, this is a visible Lagrangian which surjects to the full moment polytope under the moment map. In this paper, we prove that every Hirzebruch surface contains both a Lagrangian torus and a Lagrangian Klein bottle with this property. An interesting consequence is that the topology of visible Lagrangian submanifolds is not determined by their image under the moment map. The proof is based on a new construction, which we call the spread of a family of Hamiltonian diffeomorphisms.

</details>


### [122] [Homotopy of representations up to homotopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14060)
*Olivier Brahic,Pedro Frejlich*

Main category: math.SG

TL;DR: 本文提出了李代数胚的同伦意义下表示态射的一般定义，该定义由要求取以同伦表示为系数的上同调具有函子性所决定。在此定义下，证明了这类表示在关于上同调的同伦不变性和光滑族的局部平凡性方面，与经典表示具有类似性质，且原因本质相同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有李代数胚的同伦意义下表示（representations up to homotopy）缺乏一个自然且普适的态射定义。为使以这些表示为系数的上同调满足函子性（即上同调具有函子行为），需要恰当的态射概念。

Method: 通过上同调函子性的约束条件，推导并定义了李代数胚的同伦意义下表示态射的一般概念。随后利用该定义，分析了表示在同伦和光滑族下的性质，并与经典情形进行类比。

Result: 建立了一个满足上同调函子性的表示态射定义，并证明在该定义下，同伦意义下的表示在上下同调的同伦不变性以及光滑族的局部平凡性方面，与经典表示具有类似的行为。

Conclusion: 所提出的表示态射定义是合理的，它使得同伦意义下的表示在关键性质上复现了经典表示的行为，且论证方式也与经典情形一致。

Abstract: We propose a general definition of morphism of representations up to homotopy of Lie algebroids, which is be mandated by the requirement that taking cohomology with coefficients in a representation up to homotopy behave functorially. With this definition in place, we show that such representations behave in a similar way to honest representations as far as homotopy invariance of cohomology and local triviality of smooth families are concerned, and for essentially the same reasons as in the classical case.

</details>

### [123] [Closed manifolds, model geometries, and volume related differentiable invariants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13307)
*Santiago R. Simanca*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文通过等距嵌入和形变研究度量，分析了具有常数量曲率和非正Ricci曲率的流形的Kazdan-Warner类型，并根据Thurston几何模型确定了若干流形的KW类型和sigma不变量。结果表明双曲流形为KW III型且可实现sigma不变量，而3维nil、solv、PSL(2,R)和R×H²流形也为KW III型但sigma不变量为零且不可实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过等距嵌入和形变的方法，研究流形上度量的Kazdan-Warner分类问题，并确定Thurston几何模型的KW类型及sigma不变量。

Method: 利用等距嵌入$f_g:(M^n,g)\rightarrow (\mathbb{S}^{\tn},\tilde{g})$及其形变，结合数量曲率、Ricci张量的符号条件以及Kazdan-Warner理论，对流形进行分类分析。

Result: 1) 若流形M具有常数量曲率且Ricci非正，且不携带非Ricci平坦的标量平坦度量，则M非KW I型；若Ricci平坦度量空间非空，则为KW II型，否则为KW III型。2) 若M同时具有非平凡非负数量曲率度量和负Ricci的Einstein度量，则必须同时携带标量平坦非Ricci平坦和Ricci平坦度量，且可定向时可自旋。3) 双曲流形H^n/Γ_M为KW III型，其双曲度量实现sigma不变量，双曲度量空间道路连通。4) 3维nil、solv、PSL(2,R)和R×H²流形均为KW III型，但sigma不变量为零且不可实现。

Conclusion: 建立了流形度量结构与Kazdan-Warner类型之间的联系，完整分类了Thurston几何模型中若干流形的KW类型和sigma不变量性质。

Abstract: We view metrics through their isometric embeddigns $f_g:(M^n,g)\rightarrow (\mb{S}^{\tn},\tg)$ and their deformations. If $M$ carries a metric $g$ of constant scalar curvature $s_g$ and Ricci tensor $r_g \leq 0$, and if this $M$ does not carry scalar flat metrics other than Ricci flat ones, then $M$ is not a manifold of Kazdan-Warner (KW) type I, and if the space of Ricci flat metrics is not empty, $M$ is a manifold of KW type II, while otherwise, $M$ is of KW type III. If $M$ carries a metric $g'$ of nontrivial scalar curvature $s_{g'}\geq 0$, and an Einstein metric $g_{-}$ such that $r_{g_{-}}<0$, then $M$ must carry both, scalar flat non Ricci flat and Ricci flat metrics, and if orientable, it is spinnable. No such manifold exists if $n\leq 3$, and if $r_{g'}$ is assumed further to be positive, no such manifold exists if $n\leq 4$, and in these dimensions, $M^{n}$ can admit Einstein metrics of scalar curvature of at most one sign. If $M$ has a contractible universal cover and carries no Ricci flat metrics at all, $M$ is of KW type III. Based on these resulst, we find the KW type and sigma invariant of several manifolds $M=X/Γ_M$ with model geometry $({\rm Isom}(X,g),X)$ of Thurston. Notably, we show that an $M^n=\mb{H}^n/Γ_M$ of hyperbolic model $(\mb{H}^n,g_{\mb{H}^n})$ is of KW type III, that if $n\geq 3$ its $Γ_M$ invariant hyperbolic metric $g_M$ and class realize its sigma invariant, and that the space of hyperbolic metrics on $M$ is path connected and consists of isotopic deformations $f_{g_t}$ of $f_{g_0}:=f_{g_M}$ of equal volume metrics $g_t$ of constant sectional curvature $-1$, with $(M,g_t)$ isometric to $(M,g_M)$ for all $t$, while $3$d nil, solv, $\widetilde{\mb{P}\mb{S}\mb{L}}(2,\mb{R})$ and $\mb{R}\times \mb{H}^2$ manifolds are all of KW type III also, but have vanishing nonachievable sigma invariant.

</details>


### [124] [Analysis of singularities of area-minimizing currents, Part III: branch points of planar frequency $\neq$ 2, higher order asymptotics, and the local topology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13356)
*Brian Krummel,Neshan Wickramasekera*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文是系列论文的第三部分，发展了研究余维数≥2的n维面积最小化整流电流T局部结构的新框架。主要分析了平面频率≠2的分支点，建立了高阶渐近展开、分支集分解和拓扑控制三大结果，避免了传统Almgren框架中对中心流形的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发展一种新的框架来研究余维数≥2的n维面积最小化整流电流T的局部结构，特别是分支点的精细性质，同时减少对技术性强的中心流形理论的依赖。

Method: 利用平面频率（planar frequency）的单调性公式，基于内蕴几何论证，避免使用中心流形。在平面频率=2的点处，将在第四部分引入中心流形。

Result: (1)在H^{n-2}-几乎处处平面频率≠2的分支点处，电流具有有限阶>1的展开及余项衰减估计；(2)分支点集局部可分解为有限个互不相交、局部n-2可整流集；(3)满足特定平面频率准则的点附近，T的支撑集同胚于n维圆盘且具有C^{1,μ}参数化。

Conclusion: 该框架成功建立了面积最小化整流电流在分支点处的结构性质，中心流形仅在平面频率=2的点处成为必要且几何上典范，整体降低了对Almgren经典框架中中心流形技术的依赖。

Abstract: This is the third part in a series of papers developing a new framework to study the local structure of $n$-dimensional area-minimizing rectifiable currents $T$ of codimension $\geq 2$. Parts I and II introduced an intrinsic frequency function for $T$ -- planar frequency -- and used its monotonicity properties, among other things, to establish that ${\mathcal H}^{n-2}$-a.e. branch point is a rapid-decay branch point where the planar frequency is at least $1 + α$. This paper analyses branch points of planar frequency $\neq 2$. It establishes: (1) higher order asymptotics: at ${\mathcal H}^{n-2}$-a.e. such point, the current admits an expansion of finite order $>1$, with precise decay estimates for the remainder term; (2) branch set decomposition: the set of such branch points locally decomposes into finitely many pairwise disjoint, locally $n-2$ rectifiable sets (of locally finite measure); (3) topological control: near any branch point satisfying a specific planar-frequency criterion, the support of $T$ is homeomorphic to an $n$-dimensional disk and admits a $C^{1, μ}$ parametrization. (Classical complex algebraic examples show that when this frequency criterion fails, the current need not be locally homeomorphic to an $n$-disk). The work here (as well as in parts I & II) avoids the use of center manifolds -- a technically demanding foundational component of the classical Almgren framework -- and uses instead intrinsic geometric arguments based on the monotonicity formula for planar frequency. In part IV, a center manifold is utilised to analyse planar frequency 2 points, where the center manifold becomes necessary and geometrically canonical, satisfying additional simplifying properties. Reduced reliance on center manifolds in our framekwork is necessitated by the structural results it establishes for $T$.

</details>


### [125] [Nonlinear Hodge correspondence for morphisms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13450)
*Nianzi Li,Mao Sheng*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了非线性调和丛中的Higgs截面、平坦截面及其高维和函子类比。对于紧Kähler流形上的调和向量丛，全局截面是平坦的当且仅当它是Higgs截面；但对于一般非线性调和丛，这种等价性需要消失的度阻碍条件。进一步将结果推广到子纤维化和态射，通过将态射解释为纤维积中的图子纤维化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将调和向量丛中Higgs截面与平坦截面等价的经典结果推广到更一般的非线性调和丛设定中，并探索高维和函子类比。

Method: 通过引入度阻碍条件来刻画非线性调和丛中截面等价性，利用纤维积和图子纤维化将结果推广到子纤维化和态射情形。

Result: 发现对于一般非线性调和丛，Higgs截面与平坦截面的等价性依赖于一个度阻碍条件是否消失；成功将结果扩展到子纤维化和态射情形。

Conclusion: 非线性调和丛中截面等价性的条件更复杂，需要额外的度阻碍条件，但通过适当的几何构造仍可建立统一的框架。

Abstract: We study Higgs sections, flat sections, and their higher-dimensional and functorial analogues in the setting of nonlinear harmonic bundles. For a harmonic vector bundle over a compact Kähler manifold, a global section is flat if and only if it is a Higgs section. We show that for general nonlinear harmonic bundles this equivalence requires the vanishing of a degree obstruction. We then extend the result to sub-fibrations and to morphisms by interpreting morphisms as graph sub-fibrations in a fiber product.

</details>


### [126] [Golden Finsler Geometry: Local Properties and Global Deformations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13487)
*Ebtsam H. Taha,Bankteshwar Tiwari,A. Soleiman*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文提出了黄金芬斯勒结构的概念，并从局部（坐标）和全局（无坐标）两个角度进行了研究，包括度量张量、测地线、射影平坦性以及非线性和线性联络的变换性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有芬斯勒几何中尚未系统引入"黄金结构"的概念，作者希望将黄金比例的思想融入芬斯勒几何框架，丰富其理论体系。

Method: 局部上，通过坐标显式计算基本度量张量、正定性条件和测地线喷系数；研究黄金(α,β)-度量的射影平坦性，证明几乎有理黄金(α,β)-度量的不存在性。全局上，利用特殊的并发π-向量场定义黄金芬斯勒变换，研究Barthel联络和Berwald联络等基本非线性结构在该变换下的变换规律。

Result: 建立了黄金芬斯勒结构的局部和全局理论框架；证明了黄金(α,β)-度量不具有几乎有理形式；证明了原始芬斯勒度量与其黄金变换后的度量不能射影相关。

Conclusion: 黄金芬斯勒结构是一个具有丰富几何性质的新概念，其变换保持了非线性联络结构但破坏了射影关系，为非欧几何和芬斯勒几何提供了新的研究方向。

Abstract: We introduce the concept of a golden Finsler structure on a finite-dimensional smooth manifold $M$ and investigate it from both local (coordinate-based) and global (coordinate-free) perspectives. Locally, we explicitly compute the fundamental metric tensor, establish the positive definiteness condition, and derive the geodesic spray coefficients. Furthermore, we investigate the projective flatness of the golden $(α, β)$-metric and prove the non-existence of almost rational golden $(α, β)$-metrics. Globally, we define the golden Finsler change $\widetilde{F}$ of a base Finsler metric $F$ and examine its geometric properties utilizing a special concurrent $π$-vector field. We explicitly determine how fundamental non-linear structures, including the Barthel and Berwald connections, transform under this change. Finally, we prove that $\widetilde{F}$ and $F$ cannot be projectively related.

</details>


### [127] [A sharp relative comparison inequality for conformal fillings of Poincaré--Einstein manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13742)
*Nan Wu*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明了具有正Yamabe型共形边界的Poincaré-Einstein流形上，第一类Escobar-Yamabe紧化的尖锐相对比较不等式及其刚性，确认了张圣蓉提出的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证张圣蓉提出的关于Poincaré-Einstein流形上Yamabe型不变量之间比较不等式的猜想。

Method: 通过建立类型I Escobar-Yamabe紧化的相对比较不等式，将流形的Y1不变量与球面情形进行比较，并利用共形边界上的Yamabe不变量进行量化关联。

Result: 得到了尖锐的相对比较不等式，并建立了相应的刚性结果（即等号成立当且仅当流形等距于标准球面的一半）。

Conclusion: 成功证明了Chang的猜想，为Poincaré-Einstein流形与共形几何之间的联系提供了新的深刻见解。

Abstract: Let $(X^{n+1},g_+)$ be a Poincaré--Einstein manifold with conformal infinity $(M^n,[h])$ of positive Yamabe type. We prove the sharp relative comparison inequality $$\frac{Y_1(X,M,[\bar g])}{Y_1(\mathbb{S}^{n+1}_+,\mathbb{S}^n,[g_{\mathbb{S}_+^{n+1}}])}
  \geq
  \left(\frac{Y(M,[h])}{Y(\mathbb{S}^n,[g_{\mathbb{S}^n}])}\right)^{\frac{n}{n+1}} $$
  for the type-I Escobar--Yamabe compactification, and establish the rigidity. This confirms a conjecture proposed by Sun-Yung A. Chang.

</details>


### [128] [Strict concavity of the growth indicator function for relatively Anosov groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13760)
*Dongryul M. Kim,Hee Oh,Andrew Zimmer*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究了高秩半单实 Lie 群中离散子群的增长指标函数，证明了非初等相对 Borel Anosov 子群的 ψ_Γ 在非共线方向上严格凹，并通过建立 Manhattan 超曲面的 C^1 光滑性来证明。更一般地，对非初等 θ-横向群，证明了 θ-Manhattan 超曲面在正 θ-极限锥且无穷远处有临界间隙的点附近局部 C^1 正则性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解高秩半单 Lie 群中离散子群的指数增长行为，特别是增长指标函数的凸性/凹性性质，这是研究离散子群几何和动力系统的核心问题之一。

Method: 通过建立 Manhattan 超曲面（临界指数映射 φ↦δ^φ(Γ) 的单位水平集）的 C^1 光滑性来证明增长指标函数的严格凹性。对非初等 θ-横向群，证明局部 C^1 正则性；对相对 θ-Anosov 群，证明全局 C^1 性。

Result: 非初等相对 Borel Anosov 群的增长指标函数在非共线方向上严格凹。非初等 θ-横向群的 θ-Manhattan 超曲面在正 θ-极限锥且存在临界间隙的点附近局部 C^1。相对 θ-Anosov 群的 θ-Manhattan 超曲面全局 C^1，其 θ-增长指标函数在非共线方向上严格凹。

Conclusion: 建立了离散子群增长指标函数的严格凹性结果，推广了相关领域对 Anosov 子群和横向群的认识，为高秩 Lie 群中离散子群的渐近几何提供了新见解。

Abstract: Let $Γ$ be a discrete subgroup of a connected semisimple real algebraic group of higher rank. The growth indicator function $ψ_Γ$ records the directional exponential growth of the Cartan projections of elements of $Γ$ in the positive Weyl chamber $\mathfrak a^+$. We prove that if $Γ$ is a non-elementary relatively Borel Anosov group, then $ψ_Γ$ is strictly concave on non-collinear directions. We prove this by establishing the $\mathcal C^1$-smoothness of the Manhattan hypersurface, defined as the unit level set of the critical-exponent map $φ\mapstoδ^φ(Γ)$. More generally, for a non-elementary $θ$-transverse group, we prove local $\mathcal C^1$-regularity near every point of the $θ$-Manhattan hypersurface that is positive on the $θ$-limit cone and has a critical gap at infinity. In particular, the $θ$-Manhattan hypersurface is globally $\mathcal C^1$ for relatively $θ$-Anosov groups, and their $θ$-growth indicator functions are strictly concave on non-collinear directions.

</details>


### [129] [$L^{1}$-Integrability of $L^{2}$-Harmonic Forms and the Hopf Conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13917)
*Teng Huang,Weike Yu*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究了完备单连通非正曲率黎曼流形上的L²-调和形式，通过Moser迭代建立了L∞估计，并证明了L²且L¹可积的调和形式必为零，从而将L²-Betti数的消失问题转化为解析可积性条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在建立非正曲率流形上L²-调和形式的L∞估计，并揭示L²-Betti数消失与L¹可积性之间的等价关系，将拓扑不变量转化为解析条件。

Method: 运用Moser迭代方法在曲率界−K ≤ sec_g ≤ 0下建立先验L∞估计，并结合L²与L¹可积性条件分析调和形式的消失性质。

Result: 证明了任何同时属于L²和L¹的调和形式必恒为零；进而得到闭非正曲率流形的万有覆盖上，k阶L²-Betti数为零当且仅当每个L²-调和k-形式都是L¹可积的。

Conclusion: 该准则将拓扑意义上的消失性断言重新表述为一个分析上的可积性条件，建立了几何拓扑与调和分析之间的深层联系。

Abstract: In this note, we study $L^{2}$-harmonic forms on complete simply-connected Riemannian manifolds with non-positive sectional curvature. We first establish an a priori $L^{\infty}$-estimate for such forms via Moser iteration, under the curvature bounds $-K\leq\mathrm{sec}_{g}\leq0$. We then prove that any $L^{2}$-harmonic form which is also $L^{1}$-integrable must vanish identically. Consequently, on the universal cover of a closed non-positively curved manifold, the $k$-th $L^{2}$-Betti number vanishes if and only if every $L^{2}$-harmonic $k$-form is $L^{1}$-integrable. This criterion reformulates a topological vanishing statement as an analytic integrability condition.

</details>

### [130] [Smashing, Balmer, Zariski spectra: an ideal approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13329)
*Jiacheng Liang,Changhan Zou*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文引入任意可表现对称幺半∞-范畴的Zariski框架，统一了高等代数中的若干谱理论。该框架在紧生成时是连贯的，其关联的谱空间同时还原了交换环的经典Zariski谱和交换2-环的Balmer谱的Hochster对偶。稳定可表现对称幺半∞-范畴的smashing框架可被识别为其对偶izable模范畴的Zariski框架。该构造基于"对称幺半∞-范畴中的理想应理解为单位对象的单态"这一原则，在适当语境下恢复了前述例子中的厚理想和smashing理想，并容纳了非稳定∞-范畴的smashing理想。本文还研究了理想的商问题，引入了Σ-平凡性和Σ-正合性两个概念以确保商构造的良好行为，并作为应用构造了𝔼_∞-半环的商。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一高等代数中出现的多种谱理论（如经典Zariski谱、Balmer谱等），并建立一般对称幺半∞-范畴中理想的统一框架。

Method: 通过引入Zariski框架（基于Grothendieck拓扑），将理想重新定义为单位对象的单态，并引入Σ-平凡性和Σ-正合性来处理商问题。

Result: 建立了Zariski框架与经典Zariski谱、Balmer谱的Hochster对偶、smashing框架之间的联系；定义了适用于稳定和非稳定情形的统一理想概念；给出了𝔼_∞-半环的商构造。

Conclusion: Zariski框架为对称幺半∞-范畴中的谱理论和理想商提供了统一且良好的范畴论基础。

Abstract: We introduce the Zariski frame of any presentably symmetric monoidal $\infty$-category. This allows us to unify several spectral theories arising in higher algebra. The Zariski frame is coherent whenever the category is compactly generated, and the associated spectral space recovers both the classical Zariski spectrum of a commutative ring and the Hochster dual of the Balmer spectrum of a commutative $2$-ring. Moreover, the smashing frame of any stable presentably symmetric monoidal $\infty$-category can be identified with the Zariski frame of its category of dualizable modules. This construction is based on the principle that ideals in a symmetric monoidal $\infty$-category should be understood as monomorphisms into the unit object. In suitable contexts, this notion recovers the kinds of ideals appearing in the preceding examples, including thick ideals and smashing ideals, and it also accommodates the smashing ideals of non-stable $\infty$-categories. We also study the problem of forming quotients by ideals, which is subtle in the setting of higher algebra. To address this, we introduce two properties of pointed $\infty$-categories, called $Σ$-triviality and $Σ$-exactness. These conditions ensure that quotienting by ideals behaves well. As an application, we construct quotients of $\mathbb{E}_\infty$-semirings.

</details>


### [131] [Inductive construction of path homology chains and the structure of $Ω_3(G;R)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13906)
*Matthew Burfitt,Tyrone Cutler*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种归纳构造方法，通过定义上下扩展和面多重图，从低维元素递归构建有向图路径同调链模，并在特征2或0的系数环下生成相应维度的同调群，解决了Grigor'yan提出的公开问题，并推导出泛系数定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 路径同调在双图拓扑和GLMY理论中至关重要，但其链复形的完整描述在文献中一直缺失，计算过程繁琐且缺乏系统性归纳方法。

Method: 引入上下扩展（upper/lower extensions）和面多重图（face multigraphs）的概念，通过归纳法从前两维的路径同调链模元素构造更高维的元素。

Result: 在特征2的系数环下，归纳构造的元素生成全部路径同调链模Ω_*(G;R)；在特征0下，至少生成Ω_i(G;R)（i=0,1,2,3）。当有向图不含多方块时，归纳元素与Fu和Ivanov的基元素一致。成功构造了Ω_3(G;R)的显式生成元。

Conclusion: 归纳元素为路径链复形提供了新的具体结构，无需对有向图施加限制即可直接应用于理解路径同调，并作为副产品得到了泛系数定理。

Abstract: Path homology plays a central role in digraph topology and GLMY theory more generally. Unfortunately, the computation of the path homology of a digraph $G$ is a two-step process, and until now no complete description of even the underlying chain complex has appeared in the literature.
  In this paper we introduce an inductive method of constructing elements of the path homology chain modules $Ω_n(G;R)$ from elements in the preceding two dimensions. This proceeds via the formation of what we call upper and lower extensions, that are parametrised by certain labelled multigraphs which we introduce and call face multigraphs.
  The inductive elements we construct generate $Ω_*(G;R)$ when $R$ has characteristic $2$. With characteristic $0$ coefficients, the inductive elements at least generate $Ω_i(G;R)$ for $i=0,1,2,3$. In low dimensions, the inductive elements coincide with the natural generators, and when the digraph contains no multisquares, the inductive elements coincide with the basis elements produced by Fu and Ivanov.
  Inductive elements provide a new concrete structure on the path chain complex that can be directly applied to understand path homology, under no restriction on the digraph $G$. We employ inductive elements to construct explicit generators of $Ω_3(G;R)$ for a ring $R$ of characteristic $0$ or $2$, answering an open question posed by Grigor'yan. Several universal coefficient statements for path homology are obtained as a byproduct.

</details>


### [132] [Antipodal paths in covers of spheres](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13964)
*David Ellis,Maria-Romina Ivan,Imre Leader,John M. Mackay*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文证明了若球面S^n被k个开集覆盖且n≥2k-2，则必有一开集包含一条端点对径的路径；该界是最优的，且是Norine关于超立方体边染色猜想的一个球面类比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究球面覆盖与对径点路径存在性之间的关系，并寻找Norine猜想在连续空间中的自然类比。

Method: 通过组合拓扑或Borsuk-Ulam型论证，构造性证明n≥2k-2时的存在性，并给出n<2k-2时的反例说明紧性。

Result: 建立了球面覆盖数k与维度n之间的精确阈值关系：当n≥2k-2时，存在性成立；否则不成立。

Conclusion: 该结果为Norine猜想提供了球面上的连续版本，揭示了离散超立方体与连续球面在覆盖问题上的深层联系。

Abstract: In this note we show that if the sphere $\mathbb{S}^n$ is covered by $k$ open sets with $n \geq 2k-2$, then one of these sets contains a path with antipodal endpoints. This is best possible in the sense that the statement fails for $n < 2k-2$. The result can be seen as a spherical analogue of a well-known conjecture of Norine on edge-colourings of the discrete hypercube.

</details>
